Our results suggest that in stateful search, budget allocation should be organized around the population, not the individual call.

</details>


### [141] [Answer First, Reason Later: Commitment Order in Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05687)
*Jewon Yeom,Jaewon Sok,Seonghyeon Park,Jeongjae Park,Hwiyeong Lee,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现掩码扩散语言模型在推理任务中因"任意顺序提交token"的自由而导致失败：模型会过早提交最终答案、退化为仅输出答案的模式。通过因果干预（前沿门控提交）恢复性能，同时保留高达4倍的并行解码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLMs以"任意顺序提交token"为卖点，声称优于自回归解码，但作者发现这一自由度在推理任务中反而成为失败的根本原因。

Method: 对LLaDA-8B在GSM8K上记录每次提交；设计2x2提示-解码器实验分解交互效应（坍塌通道与顺序通道）；提出"前沿门控提交"（frontier-gated commitment）单旋钮干预方法，在Dream-7B和MATH-500上复现验证。

Result: 无约束解码在轨迹15-24%处就提交最终答案，且画布增大时90%的问题退化为仅答案输出；链式思维仅在有序提交下有效（+34.8个百分点）；前沿门控干预将性能从0.528恢复到0.852，同时保持最多4倍并行解码。

Conclusion: 现有窗口式采样器（此前被解释为效率动机）本质上是对推理病态的最小修复，尽管它们从未为此设计。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) can commit tokens in any order -- a freedom marketed as their core advantage over autoregressive decoding. We show that on reasoning tasks this freedom is instead the axis of failure. Logging every commitment during decoding of LLaDA-8B on GSM8K, we find that unconstrained (pure) decoding commits the final answer at 15-24% of the trajectory while half the reasoning region is still masked, and collapses to answer-only outputs on up to 90% of problems as the canvas grows. The cause is not the model's termination beliefs -- EOS "pressure" is nearly identical across decoders -- but reachability: whether the sampler may act on those beliefs at distant positions. A 2x2 prompt-decoder design shows that chain-of-thought helps only under ordered commitment (interaction +34.8 percentage points, 95% CI [26.8, 42.8]; without reasoning text the decoders are indistinguishable), an interaction we decompose into a collapse channel and an order channel and replicate on Dream-7B and MATH-500. A single-knob intervention -- frontier-gated commitment -- causally recovers the full gap (0.528 to 0.852) while preserving up to 4x parallel decoding, along a measured frontier whose optimal window flips from w=1 at full refinement to unconstrained at 8 tokens/step. Our results reframe existing window-style samplers, previously motivated by efficiency, as the minimal fix for a reasoning pathology they were never designed to address.

</details>


### [142] [Sparse Mutual Information Graph Averaging for Improving Random Indexing Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05724)
*Sriram Loganathan,Gokul Anand,Aung Bo Bo,Yourui Shao,William B. Andreopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究基于稀疏全局语料统计的随机索引（RI）词嵌入方法，通过在稀疏PPMI图上进行top-K加权平均来修复弱RI初始化。在童话故事语料库的272个Google家族类类比问题上，该方法将准确率从19.4±0.7%提升到30.7±2.9%（最好种子达到34.6%），但该方法在text8上无法与神经基线竞争，且在SimLex-999上严格相似性相关性近乎为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的稀疏词嵌入管道需要避免稠密共现矩阵的物化、稠密分解和梯度训练，本文旨在探索一种仅依赖稀疏全局语料统计、无需梯度训练的词嵌入修复方法，特别关注能否通过PPMI图上的加权平均来改善弱RI嵌入的质量。

Method: 使用Random Indexing (RI)向量作为初始化嵌入，在稀疏的Positive Pointwise Mutual Information (PPMI)图上进行top-K邻域加权平均来精炼嵌入。在童话故事语料库上评估家族类类比任务，并与PPMI+SVD、Binary+SVD、CBOW和Skip-gram等基线方法对比，同时在text8和SimLex-999上进行额外评估，并测试了Bloom filter草图方案。

Result: 在童话故事语料库的272个谷歌家族类类比问题上，PPMI top-K图平均将RI准确率从19.4±0.7%提高到30.7±2.9%（五个种子），最佳种子seed42达到34.6%。但同样的邻域平均方法会降低PPMI+SVD、Binary+SVD、CBOW和Skip-gram的类比准确率。在text8上无法与神经基线竞争，在SimLex-999上严格相似性相关性几乎为零。Bloom filter草图在测试配置下表现不如RI。

Conclusion: PPMI图平均结合top-K剪枝是一种有效的无梯度修复方法，能够显著改善弱RI嵌入的类比推理能力，但其效果仅限于RI这类弱嵌入，不适用于更强的神经或SVD基线，且泛化性有限（在text8和SimLex-999上表现不佳）。该方法缺乏与神经基线的竞争力，但作为非梯度修复路径仍有研究价值。

Abstract: Sparse word embedding pipelines can avoid dense co-occurrence matrix materialization, dense factorization, and gradient training while still relying on sparse global corpus statistics. This paper studies Random Indexing (RI) vectors refined by weighted averaging on a sparse Positive Pointwise Mutual Information (PPMI) graph. On a fairytales corpus, the covered semantic analogy set consists of 272 Google family- category questions. On this family subset, PPMI top-K graph averaging repairs a weak RI initialization, improving accuracy from 19.4+-0.7% to 30.7+-2.9% across five seeds. Under the single tested runs, the same neighborhood averaging reduces family- subset analogy accuracy for PPMI+SVD (singular value decom- position), Binary+SVD, CBOW, and Skip-gram. Thus the method is not competitive with neural baselines on text8 and gives near- zero strict similarity correlation on SimLex-999. While Bloom filter sketches underperform RI in the tested configuration, we find that PPMI graph averaging with top-K pruning is a useful non-gradient repair for weak RI embeddings. On the fairytales dataset, PPMI top-K=50 graph averaging improves RI with accuracy going from 19.4+-0.7% to 30.7+-2.9%, and performing best with a seed42 of 34.6%.

</details>


### [143] [Mitigating Scoring Bias in LLM-as-a-Judge via Random Number Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05726)
*Yuma Asato,Kiyoaki Shirai,Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过测量LLM在随机数字生成中的潜在数字偏差来校准其评估分数的方法，在四个任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评估器（LLM-as-a-Judge）时会倾向于生成特定分数而不考虑被评估文本的实际内容，即评分偏差（scoring bias），这严重影响评估的可靠性。

Method: 让LLM随机生成数字token，通过测量观测数字分布与均匀分布的偏差来识别其潜在数字偏差；将下游任务定义加入随机数字生成的提示中，以测量任务特定的潜在数字偏差；最后在评估时利用LLM的token生成概率结合潜在数字偏差进行校正。

Result: 在LLM对齐评估、摘要评估、语义文本相似度和语义文本相关性四个任务上的实验表明，所提方法优于无去偏的LLM以及之前的校准方法；同时发现评分偏差因LLM、任务和分数范围而异。

Conclusion: 测量LLM的潜在数字偏差对校准评分偏差具有重要意义，且需要根据具体情况（不同的模型、任务和分数范围）进行个性化测量与校正。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are often used as evaluators of text quality, known as LLM-as-a-Judge, which can outperform conventional automatic evaluation metrics that rely on reference texts. However, LLM evaluators tend to generate particular scores regardless of the context of the evaluated text, which is known as scoring bias. This study proposes a novel method to mitigate this scoring bias. An LLM is instructed to randomly generate number tokens, and the latent numerical bias of the LLM is identified by measuring the deviation of the observed distribution of numbers from the uniform distribution. A definition of a downstream task, for which an LLM evaluator is used, is added to the prompts for random number generation to measure task-specific latent number bias. In the evaluation by an LLM, the token generation probabilities for a given input are rectified considering the LLM's latent number bias. Results of the experiment on four different tasks, evaluation of LLM alignment, evaluation of summarization, Semantic Textual Similarity, and Semantic Textual Relatedness, demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms the baselines, including an LLM without debiasing and previous calibration methods. In addition, it is confirmed that scoring bias varies across LLMs, tasks, and score ranges, indicating the importance of measuring latent number bias as the case may be.

</details>


### [144] [Once a Response, Always a Response: Detecting LLM-generated Text via Latent Prompt Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05741)
*Hongrui Bao,Yubing Ren,Yanan Cao,Jinhan You,Fang Fang,Shi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoPrompt，一种基于潜在提示恢复的训练无关零样本机器文本检测器，通过预置通用前缀来重新激活机器生成文本与上游提示之间的隐藏依赖关系，测量并校准似然增益差异以量化检测分数，在多个测试场景中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的文本在规模上日益增多，带来虚假信息传播、教育滥用和平台治理等风险。现有零样本检测器主要利用基于概率的统计差异，但未显式考虑LLM的训练过程，导致其独特的生成机制未被充分建模，检测鲁棒性受限。

Method: EchoPrompt提出三个核心步骤：首先，通过预置统一的通用助手-回复前缀来部分重新激活机器生成文本与上游提示之间的隐藏依赖；其次，使用指令微调模型测量由此诱导的似然增益；然后，将该似然增益与对应的基础模型进行校准；最后，聚合两者的差异作为量化潜在提示依赖性的检测分数。

Result: 大量实验表明，EchoPrompt在零样本检测器中达到最先进的性能，并在具有挑战性的评估设置下保持强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模LLM训练过程中的生成机制——即文本对上游提示的条件依赖，EchoPrompt有效弥补了现有概率统计型检测器的不足，为机器生成文本检测提供了更鲁棒且无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent and convincing text at scale, creating growing risks for misinformation dissemination, educational misuse, and platform governance. These concerns make robust detection of machine-generated text increasingly necessary. Recent zero-shot detectors mainly exploit probability-based statistical discrepancies, but they do not explicitly account for the training process of LLMs, which leaves a distinct generation mechanism insufficiently modeled and limits detection robustness. To address this issue, we propose EchoPrompt, a training-free detector based on latent prompt restoration. Our key intuition is that machine-generated text is typically produced conditioned on an upstream prompt, and this hidden dependency can be partially reactivated by prepending a unified generic prefix. Specifically, EchoPrompt restores a generic assistant-response context, measures the induced likelihood gain with an instruction-tuned model, calibrates it against the corresponding base model, and aggregates the resulting differences into a score that quantifies latent prompt dependency. Extensive experiments show that EchoPrompt achieves state-of-the-art performance among zero-shot detectors while maintaining strong robustness across challenging evaluation settings.

</details>


### [145] [How to Recognize New Words: A Comparison Between Context Biasing Methods and Speech LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05759)
*Christian Huber,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种ASR处理新词和罕见词（命名实体、缩写词、领域特殊词汇等）的策略：上下文偏置方法（在推理时提供词表扩展ASR模型）与带上下文提示的语音大语言模型（speech LLM）。基于Whisper的两种上下文偏置方法最多可将偏置词错误率相对降低88%，且对其他词影响甚微；语音LLM在朗读语音上表现优异，但在非朗读语音上泛化较差，且对干扰词数量和提示词顺序敏感。文章刻画了两种方法的权衡，为方法选择提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别训练数据中稀缺的新词和罕见词（如命名实体、缩写词、领域特殊词）仍是自动语音识别（ASR）的关键挑战，需要对不同解决策略进行系统比较。

Method: 在朗读和非朗读语音上，比较两种基于Whisper的上下文偏置方法与三种语音LLM（直接以上下文提示），报告偏置、非偏置及总体词错误率（WER）等多维度指标。

Result: 上下文偏置方法可将偏置词WER相对降低高达88%，且对非偏置词影响极小；语音LLM在朗读语音上表现优异，但在非朗读语音上泛化不足，且对干扰词数量和提示词顺序敏感。

Conclusion: 两种策略各有优劣：上下文偏置方法稳健且对非目标词影响小，语音LLM在朗读场景表现更好但对非朗读场景和提示配置敏感，需根据实际应用场景权衡选择。

Abstract: Recognizing new and rare words - named entities, acronyms, domain specific special words, and other items scarce in training data - remains a key challenge for automatic speech recognition (ASR). We compare two strategies for this: context biasing methods, where an ASR model is extended such that during inference a word list can be supplied, and speech large language models (LLMs) prompted with context directly. We evaluate two context biasing methods based on Whisper against three speech LLMs across read and non-read speech, reporting biased, unbiased, and overall word error rate (WER). The context biasing methods cut biased WER by up to 88% relative while leaving other words largely unaffected. Speech LLMs excel on read speech but generalize less well to non-read speech, and prove sensitive to distractor count and prompt word order. We characterize the resulting trade-offs to guide method selection.

</details>


### [146] [Task-Conditional Flow Matching for Balanced Multilingual Text Embedding Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05785)
*Tirth Bhatt,Naren Kumar S,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务条件流匹配（TCFM）框架，针对多语言文本嵌入模型在不同任务上需要不同优化策略的问题，将流匹配应用于翻译任务，同时为检索、分类等任务选择更匹配的优化目标，并在Indic Massiv文本嵌入基准上取得最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言文本嵌入模型使用单一训练目标适配各种不同任务，但不同任务本质上需要不同的优化策略，这限制了模型的整体嵌入质量。

Method: TCFM框架选择性将Flow Matching应用于翻译任务，对检索、分类和配对分类任务使用与各自学习动态更匹配的优化目标；同时结合教师引导的表征保持和三轮课程学习策略，实现稳定的适配过程。

Result: 在Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark上取得新的最先进性能，在多类多语言任务中持续提升嵌入质量，并能在不同嵌入模型家族间泛化。

Conclusion: 任务条件化的训练策略能够有效提升多语言嵌入模型的性能，该框架具有跨模型家族的泛化能力，代码和数据集将在论文被接收后公开。

Abstract: Multilingual text embedding models are commonly adapted using a single training objective across diverse tasks, despite different tasks requiring fundamentally different optimization strategies. We introduce Task-Conditional Flow Matching (TCFM), a multilingual embedding adaptation framework that selectively applies Flow Matching to translation tasks while optimizing retrieval, classification, and pair-classification tasks with objectives better aligned to their learning dynamics. TCFM further combines teacher-guided representation preservation with a three-stage curriculum to enable stable adaptation. Evaluated on the Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, TCFM establishes a new state-of-the-art, consistently improving embedding quality across a diverse set of multilingual tasks and generalizing across embedding model families. We will publicly release the codebase and datasets upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [147] [On-Policy Delta Distillation for Multilingual Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05802)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在英语、韩语和日语三种语言下，用于数学推理的On-Policy Distillation (OPD)及其增强变体OPD²。实验表明OPD²在韩语和日语上显著优于原始OPD，并缩小了英语与韩语之间的性能差距；此外，仅用英语数据进行OPD也能提升韩语和日语的表现，但会导致回答偏向英语，凸显了多语言数据的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD作为LLM后训练中强化学习的有前景替代方案正在兴起，但其在多语言环境下的有效性尚未得到充分探索，作者希望填补这一研究空白。

Method: 研究使用Qwen3模型，在英语、韩语和日语三种语言的数学推理任务上，对比原始OPD和进阶变体OPD²的性能。OPD²通过利用后训练教师模型与其基础模型之间的概率差距作为学习信号来改进OPD。

Result: OPD²在所有语言上始终优于原始OPD，尤其在韩语和日语上提升显著，并普遍缩小了英语与韩语之间的性能差距。此外，仅用英语数据进行OPD也能提升韩语和日语性能，但可能导致回答偏向英语。

Conclusion: 多语言数据对于在OPD框架下保持目标语言响应至关重要；OPD²在多语言数学推理场景下是比原始OPD更优的后训练策略。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) is emerging as a promising alternative to reinforcement learning for LLM post-training, yet its effectiveness in multilingual settings remains underexplored. We study OPD and its advanced variant, On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$), for mathematical reasoning in English, Korean, and Japanese. OPD$^2$ improves OPD by using the probability gap between a post-trained teacher and its base model as the learning signal. Experiments with Qwen3 show that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms the original OPD, with particularly strong improvements in Korean and Japanese, and generally narrows the English-Korean performance gap. We further find that English-only OPD can also increase performance for Korean and Japanese, but often shifts the responses toward English, highlighting the importance of multilingual data to preserving target-language responses.

</details>


### [148] [Hierarchical Latent Prediction for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05806)
*Chang Shi,Tim Pearce,Manan Tomar,Siddhartha Sen,John Langford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了分层潜在预测（HiLP）方法，通过引入更高级别的抽象潜在变量来辅助预测，以减少潜在空间 rollout 中的误差累积效应，显著提升长程推理和规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Next-Token Prediction（NTP）使用教师强制训练范式，可能在长程推理和规划任务上不是最优的。现有的多Token预测（MTP）和Next-Latent预测（NextLat）等方法虽然试图缓解该问题，但存在有限视野或由于多步rollout导致误差累积的问题。

Method: 提出了Hierarchical Latent Prediction（HiLP）方法，引入一个辅助的更高层次抽象潜在变量，通过分层预测机制减少潜在空间rollout中的误差累积效应，从而实现更长范围的连贯预测。

Result: 实验表明HiLP能够产生更长范围的连贯信念状态表示，在编码和多步推理基准测试中展现了有效性，同时提供了更高的推测解码效率。

Conclusion: HiLP通过分层潜在预测有效解决了现有方法在长程推理任务中的局限，不仅提升了推理性能，还带来了推测解码效率的额外优势。

Abstract: While standard Next-Token Prediction (NTP) lays the foundation of language model pre- training, its teacher-forced training paradigm may not be optimal for long-horizon reasoning and planning. Recent works such as Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) and Next-Latent prediction (NextLat) try to mitigate the problem through predicting multiple future tokens and self-supervised prediction in the latent space. However, those auxiliary objectives either have a limited horizon or suffer from compounding error from multi-step rollout. We introduce Hierarchical Latent Prediction (HiLP), which introduces an auxiliary higher-level abstract latent to help reduce the error accumulation effect in latent-space rollouts. Experiments show that HiLP can lead to longer-horizon coherent belief state representation and demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across coding and multi-step reasoning benchmarks, and offers more speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [149] [M$^3$R-Bench: A Unified Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Metaphor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05817)
*Hong Jiang,Junnan Zhu,Jingwang Huang,Xiao Sun,Yuming Yang,Jiang Zhong,Ruirui Chen,Jingman Shi,Hao Wu,Nayu Liu,Xinyi Jiang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了M³R-Bench，一个统一且带证据支撑的多模态隐喻理解基准，包含1,000个图文实例及人工验证标注，覆盖隐喻存在性、目标-源映射、情感和分阶段解释。同时提出M³R-Reasoner模型，通过课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习，使8B参数的模型在多项指标上超越更大的专有MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐喻理解基准主要通过孤立的子任务进行评估，缺乏基于证据的解释，难以判断模型是否真正建立了基于视觉和文本线索的映射，因此需要统一且证据支撑的基准来全面评估多模态隐喻理解能力。

Method: 构建M³R-Bench基准，基于概念隐喻理论和非字面语言理解理论，提供隐喻发生、目标-源映射、情感及分阶段解释（证据识别→映射建立→情感推断）的联合标注。在此基础上提出M³R-Reasoner，结合课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习来使模型推理与隐喻解释对齐。

Result: 仅以8B参数为骨干的M³R-Reasoner在四项统一任务指标上超越更大的专有MLLM；在视觉证据和情感论证分数上分别比GPT-5.5高出28.45和30.11分，在平均评分标准分数上超越Claude-Sonnet-4.6达8.00分。

Conclusion: 现有模型在多模态隐喻理解中常忽略视觉证据、依赖表面文本线索并产生不准确的目标-源映射，存在跨模态证据-映射不匹配问题；通过专门的推理对齐训练，小参数模型也能在该任务上达到甚至超越大型专有模型的性能。

Abstract: Metaphor enables the understanding of abstract concepts through cross-domain mappings while conveying affective attitudes. In multimodal scenarios, visual and textual information jointly construct Target--Source mappings, requiring both conceptual understanding and cross-modal reasoning. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate metaphor understanding through isolated subtasks and lack evidence-grounded explanations, making it difficult to assess whether models establish mappings grounded in visual and textual cues.To address these limitations, we introduce M$^3$R-Bench, a unified and evidence-grounded benchmark containing 1,000 image--text instances with human-verified annotations. Guided by Conceptual Metaphor Theory and theories of nonliteral language understanding, M$^3$R-Bench provides joint annotations for metaphor occurrence, Target--Source mapping, sentiment, and stage-wise explanations following ``evidence identification--mapping establishment--sentiment inference.''Evaluations on M$^3$R-Bench reveal that existing models often overlook visual evidence, rely on superficial textual cues, and produce inaccurate Target--Source mappings, exposing a cross-modal evidence--mapping mismatch. To address this mismatch, we propose M$^3$R-Reasoner, which combines curriculum-based reasoning supervision with task-aware reinforcement learning to align model reasoning with metaphor interpretation. Experiments show that, with only an 8B-parameter backbone, M$^3$R-Reasoner outperforms larger proprietary MLLMs across four unified-task metrics and improves Visual Evidence and Sentiment Justification scores over GPT-5.5 by 28.45 and 30.11 points, respectively, while surpassing Claude-Sonnet-4.6 by 8.00 points in mean rubric score. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hongshi4/M3R-Bench.

</details>


### [150] [Decomposed Entailment for Factuality Checking and Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05823)
*Achir Oukelmoun,Nasredine Semmar,Gaël De Chalendar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HallDetect是一个轻量级、免参考、黑盒的幻觉检测框架，通过将生成内容分解为原子声明，并由紧凑的编码器蕴含模型结合多尺度源块对比验证，在低资源条件下优于同类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型常因幻觉（生成内容与源不符）而不可靠，现有检测方法在高资源需求或性能上存在不足，需要一种轻量、高效且可泛化的幻觉检测方案。

Method: 将生成内容分解为原子声明，使用紧凑的编码器蕴含模型通过对比式方法在多层级的源块库中进行验证，并以非对称评分聚合结果——单个高置信被驳斥的声明即判定为幻觉。

Result: 在四个基准中的三个上，HallDetect优于同等资源的生成式和基于嵌入的基线，且在骨干模型家族间保持稳定，并提供了声明到文本区间的审计轨迹以定位每个错误。

Conclusion: HallDetect在受控的4位量化骨干和消费级硬件预算下展现出优越的幻觉检测性能，兼具稳定性与可解释性，为资源受限场景下的幻觉检测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often compromised by factual inconsistencies, including hallucinations---cases where generated content is not supported by the underlying source. We present HallDetect, a lightweight, reference-free, and black-box framework for hallucination detection that we evaluate not only on summarization but across a broader range of source-grounded generation settings. HallDetect builds on decomposition-based factuality evaluation: generated content is decomposed into atomic claims, each verified by a compact encoder-based entailment model through a contrastive formulation over a multi-scale library of source chunks, and aggregated with an asymmetric score in which a single confidently contradicted claim flags the response. Under a controlled protocol in which all methods share the same 4-bit quantized backbones and consumer-grade hardware budget, HallDetect outperforms comparably resourced generative and embedding-based baselines on three of four benchmarks while remaining stable across backbone families, and yields a claim-to-span audit trail that localizes each error.

</details>


### [151] [MoCA: Implicit Social Context Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05825)
*Wenhao Xu,Kaiwen Zhang,Hao Li,Maowei You,Yongzheng Ji,Siyuan Zuo,Jingxuan Yu,Sina A,Xinyao Tan,Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式社会情境分析任务（MoCA）及其基准数据集，包含3,108个多模态实例，从情感、意图和立场三个维度系统建模隐式社交场景；并提出冲突驱动的溯因推理框架（CoDAR），通过建模观察表达与期望真实行为之间的认知冲突来推断隐藏心理状态，实验表明CoDAR显著提升模型性能，但距离人类推理仍有较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社交沟通中的情感与意图往往通过隐式、间接且基于社会文化背景的信号传达，而非直接陈述。这种隐式社会语境在真实交互中普遍存在，但目前缺乏一个正式且系统的框架来研究这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出MoCA任务，从情感（affection）、意图（intent）和立场（stance）三个关键维度系统建模隐式社会场景；2) 构建包含3,108个真实世界来源的多模态实例的高质量基准数据集，配有细粒度认知标注（谁向谁表达什么、如何表达及为何表达）；3) 提出CoDAR框架，通过建模观察表达与期望真实行为之间的差异作为认知冲突，进而推断隐藏的心理状态。

Result: 现有最先进的多模态大语言模型在MoCA任务上表现困难，因为它们依赖显式线索且对潜在社会情境的推理能力有限；CoDAR框架显著提升了模型性能，但模型与人类推理之间仍存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 隐式社会理解仍然是多模态大语言模型面临的根本性难题，CoDAR的改进效果证明了冲突驱动推理范式在隐式社会推理中的有效性，但当前模型与人类之间仍有明显鸿沟，凸显了该任务的难度与未来研究空间。

Abstract: Human social communication, such as affection and intent, is often conveyed in highly implicit ways, where underlying meanings are expressed through indirect, socially and culturally grounded signals rather than explicit statements. Such implicit social contexts are pervasive in real-world interactions, yet there remains a lack of a formal and systematic framework for studying them. In this paper, we introduce Implicit Social Context Analysis (MoCA), a novel task that systematically models implicit social scenarios along three key dimensions: affection, intent, and stance. We construct a high-quality benchmark containing 3,108 multimodal instances collected from real-world sources, with fine-grained cognitive annotations revealing who expresses what toward whom, as well as how and why it is conveyed. Using the MoCA dataset, we show that state-of-the-art multimodal large language models struggle significantly with this task because of their reliance on explicit cues and limited ability to reason over latent social contexts. To address this challenge, we propose Conflict-Driven Abductive Reasoning (CoDAR), a novel framework that models the discrepancy between observed expressions and expected truthful behavior as cognitive conflict, thereby enabling the inference of hidden mental states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoDAR substantially improves model performance. Nevertheless, a large gap from human reasoning remains, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of implicit social understanding.

</details>


### [152] [Enhancing Social Intelligence in LLMs with Hierarchical Reasoning and Utterance-Level Goal Rewarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05832)
*Xiaofeng Wang,Kakam Chong,Shuai Xiao,DeXin Kong,Qingyuan Tian,Chen Ju,Xu Yan,Shuai Zhao,Fei Huang,Rui Wang,Shuguang Han,jufeng chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TSR框架，将社交对话分解为高层策略规划与低层语言执行两个层级，并引入LHRL-VGR算法通过方差门控奖励动态分配奖励信号。在SOTOPIA基准上，使用Qwen2.5-7B微调后的智能体在目标完成成功率上超越GPT-4o基准7.32%，达到多智能体社交协商任务的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在结构化任务中表现出色，但在需要长期目标协调和快速适应的动态社交互动中表现不佳。现有方法对所有话语施加统一的目标导向奖励，忽视了每一轮对话中目标的特殊性，也未能考虑潜在策略的合理性依据。

Method: 受计划行为理论启发，提出TSR（Think-Strategy-Response）框架，将社交对话分解为高层策略规划和低层语言执行两个层次。为优化该框架，引入LHRL-VGR（线性化分层强化学习与方差门控奖励）算法，该算法基于目标达成分数的方差动态路由奖励，在目标完成与策略遵循之间进行平衡。

Result: 在SOTOPIA基准上的实验表明，该方法的Qwen2.5-7B智能体在目标完成成功率上超越GPT-4o基线7.32%，在多智能体社交协商任务中达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 分层策略规划与方差门控奖励机制能够有效提升LLM在动态社交互动中的目标完成能力，表明针对不同对话轮次的目标特异性奖励分配比统一奖励更有效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel in structured tasks but struggle with dynamic social interactions, where success requires long-term goal coordination and rapid adaptation. Current methods often apply uniform goal-based rewards to every utterance, overlooking the specificity of objectives at each dialogue turn and failing to account for the rationale of potential strategies. Inspired by the Theory of Planned Behavior, we propose the Think-Strategy-Response (TSR) framework, which decomposes social dialogue into two hierarchical stages: high-level strategic planning and low-level linguistic execution. To optimize TSR, we introduce Linearized Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Variance-Gated Rewards (LHRL-VGR), a novel algorithm that dynamically routes rewards - balancing goal completion and strategy adherence - based on the variance of goal achievement scores. Experiments on the SOTOPIA benchmark show that our approach fine-tunes a Qwen2.5-7B agent to surpass the GPT-4o baseline by 7.32% in goal completion success, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance in multi-agent social negotiation tasks.

</details>


### [153] [MameLoshnLM: Yiddish Language Model and Evaluation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05850)
*Uri Katz,Omer Goldman,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Reut Tsarfaty,Noah A. Smith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MameLoshnLM，这是首个专为意第绪语构建的开源8B参数语言模型。作者引入了高质量预训练语料库Oytser和多任务基准Kashes，通过在Llama 3.1 8B上继续预训练得到该模型。实验表明MameLoshnLM在多项任务上优于同规模开放基线，且更好地捕捉了意第绪语的词汇和形态特征，为低资源语言建模提供了实用模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意第绪语虽有丰富的文本传统，但数字化资源稀缺，现有多语言语料库和基准存在大量噪声、机器翻译和错误分类文本，严重制约了该语言建模的发展。需要构建高质量、针对性的数据资源和评测基准来推动意第绪语NLP研究。

Method: 构建了两个关键资源：Oytser（结合当代网络来源与文学材料的高质量意第绪语预训练语料库）和Kashes（涵盖翻译、语言分析、信息抽取和语言理解的多任务基准）。基于这些资源，在Llama 3.1 8B上继续预训练得到MameLoshnLM。

Result: 在Kashes基准的全部任务中，MameLoshnLM优于同规模开放基线模型。定量优势之外，该模型在词汇和形态模式上更准确地捕捉意第绪语的语言特征，而通用多语言模型在这方面存在明显不足，揭示了噪声网络规模多语言数据对低资源语言的失败模式。

Conclusion: MameLoshnLM为意第绪语NLP奠定了坚实基础，同时为历史上丰富但数字化代表性不足的语言提供了实用的语言模型开发模板。研究还指出，依赖噪声的大规模多语言数据对低资源语言是失效的，需要针对性的高质量资源建设。

Abstract: We present MameLoshnLM, the first open-source 8B-parameter language model built specifically for Yiddish. Despite Yiddish's rich textual tradition, its limited digital presence and the scarcity of reliable evaluation resources have constrained progress in Yiddish language modeling. Existing multilingual corpora and benchmarks are often poor proxies for the language, containing substantial amounts of noisy, machine-translated, and misclassified text. We address these gaps by introducing Oytser, a high-quality Yiddish pretraining corpus that combines contemporary web-native sources with literary materials, and Kashes, a multi-task benchmark spanning translation, linguistic analysis, information extraction, and language understanding. Using these resources, we continue pretraining Llama 3.1 8B to obtain MameLoshnLM. Across the tasks in the benchmark, MameLoshnLM outperforms open baselines of similar scale. Our analyses show that these gains are not only quantitative: relative to general-purpose multilingual models, MameLoshnLM better captures language-defining lexical and morphological patterns, pointing to a broader failure mode of noisy web-scale multilingual data for low-resource languages. Our results provide both a foundation for Yiddish NLP and a practical template for language model development in historically rich but digitally underrepresented languages.

</details>


### [154] [Mapping Similarity Spaces across Embedding Models with Synthetic Query Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05857)
*Marcin Rozmus,Peter van der Putten*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Synthetic Query Probing（SQP）方法，通过从文档生成查询来创建受控的查询-文本块对，从而在不依赖参考标签的情况下分析不同嵌入模型之间相似度分数的分布关系。实验表明模型间的绝对分数存在系统性偏差，学习到的映射函数（尤其是等距回归）可以部分对齐这些空间并提高阈值可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成系统依赖相似度分数检索相关内容，但不同嵌入模型的分数因几何属性不同而不可直接比较，这阻碍了模型迁移和阈值复用。现有方法缺乏大规模、无参考的跨模型相似度行为分析框架。

Method: 提出Synthetic Query Probing（SQP）：从文档自动生成查询，构建受控的查询-文本块对，从而大规模获取跨模型的相似度分数对。使用线性、等距（isotonic）和分位数三种映射函数学习分数转换，并在SciFact和专有语料库上评估。

Result: 多个嵌入配置下，模型在排序方面大体一致，但绝对分数存在系统性失真。学习到的映射函数可部分对齐分数空间并改善阈值可移植性，其中等距回归效果最佳。

Conclusion: 跨模型校准是必要的，SQP可作为分析嵌入可比性的可扩展框架，为模型迁移和阈值复用提供实用支持。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems rely on similarity scores to retrieve relevant content, yet scores are not directly comparable across embedding models due to differing geometric properties, complicating model migration and limiting threshold reuse. We study how similarity scores can be related by learning mappings between score distributions rather than embeddings. We introduce Synthetic Query Probing, generating queries from documents to create controlled query-chunk pairs, enabling large-scale, reference-free analysis of cross-model similarity behavior. We evaluate the approach on multiple embedding configurations and learn score conversion functions using linear, isotonic, and quantile mappings. Experiments on SciFact and a proprietary corpus show that while models largely agree on rankings, their absolute scores exhibit systematic distortions. Learned mappings partially align these spaces and improve threshold portability, with isotonic regression performing best. Our results highlight the need for cross-model calibration and position Synthetic Query Probing as a scalable framework for analyzing embedding comparability.

</details>


### [155] [MACRO: Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05872)
*Paweł Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Akylgali Aitaza,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MACRO框架，将Transformer层的动态路由建模为基于马尔可夫链的策略，在不修改模型参数的情况下学习任务特定路由，通过top-k Viterbi算法解码高概率路由程序，在多个LLM上平均提升5.0%准确率，相比最优动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升7.2%且路由搜索时间减少9.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM逐层顺序执行，而动态层路由（如重复、跳跃等）可以提升性能，但现有方法要么需要更新模型权重、要么需要昂贵的逐实例搜索、要么推理时需要ground-truth标签，缺乏高效且无需修改参数的路由方案。

Method: MACRO将层路由建模为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，条件包括层索引、计算预算阶段、方向位移和算子上下文，支持跳跃、重复和残差隐藏状态加法操作。通过训练数据反馈更新马尔可夫路由分布，并使用top-k Viterbi算法解码高概率候选程序。

Result: 在多个推理和知识基准上对多种开放权重LLM进行评估，相比未路由基线平均提升5.0%准确率，小模型提升最大；相比最优动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升7.2%，路由搜索时间从14.8小时减少到1.6小时（减少9.4倍）。

Conclusion: MACRO证明了无需修改模型参数的马尔可夫链层路由方法可以高效提升LLM性能，尤其在资源受限的小模型上收益显著，同时大幅降低路由搜索成本，为动态层路由提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard Large Language Models (LLMs) execute layers sequentially. Dynamic layer routing, i.e. search for a different execution path through layers involving layer repetitions, skips and other moves, can improve performance. Existing routing approaches often require updating model weights, running expensive search loops per test instance, or demand ground-truth labels during inference. In this work, we propose Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers (MACRO), a framework that learns task-specific routes over LLM architectures without modifying underlying parameters. MACRO models layer routing as a context-dependent Markov policy conditioned on layer indices, computation budget phases, directional displacements, and operator context, supporting skip, repeat, and residual hidden-state addition operations. The Markov route distribution is updated via feedback on training data and decoded using a top-k Viterbi algorithm to isolate high-probability candidate programs. We evaluate MACRO across diverse reasoning and knowledge benchmarks on multiple open-weight LLMs. MACRO achieves a +5.0% average accuracy improvement over the unrouted baselines, with largest gains on small models. We outperform the best dynamic routing approach Dr. LLM by +7.2%, while reducing route-search time 9.4x (from 14.8 to 1.6 hours). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/MACRO.

</details>


### [156] [Causal Episodic Memory for Feedback-Driven Agent Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05906)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Tam Minh Chu,Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Thuyen Vinh Ha Bui,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT，一种无需训练的Text-to-SQL修复智能体，通过维护在线双极性记忆（包含已验证的修复方向和失败的尝试方向）来提升跨查询的修复效果。实验表明在Spider和BIRD基准上比无状态迭代修复分别提升约3.45%和1.09%的执行准确率，但增益的统计显著性因基准而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在修复失败时常常丢弃成功的修正方案，导致后续轮次重复发现类似的解决方案，造成计算资源浪费且修复效率低下。作者希望探寻在不更新模型参数的前提下，利用已完成修复的最终结果来改进后续Text-to-SQL修复过程的可行性。

Method: MERIT维护在线双极性记忆库，包含oracle验证的修复记忆和观察到的失败方向。采用确定性分类器对粗粒度失败类型进行分类，并据此条件化混合词法-稠密检索器，在冻结模型生成每次修订前从早期已完成episode中检索相关记忆。

Result: 使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，在相同初始预测和修复预算下，MERIT在Spider上将执行准确率从66.34%提升至69.79%，在BIRD上从47.35%提升至48.44%。配对分析显示Spider增益证据充分，BIRD增益较弱。与无类型动态检索相比，MERIT在任一基准上均未可靠分离。消融实验表明负记忆贡献有限，类型条件和词法-稠密排序的价值因数据集而异，schema局部经验提供最一致的收益。

Conclusion: 跨查询因果记忆在Text-to-SQL修复中的价值取决于数据集特性，schema局部的记忆经验最为可靠有效，而更广泛的记忆表示（如Reflexion风格）在某些场景下可能更优，尽管推理成本显著更高。

Abstract: LLM agents that repair failures often discard successful corrections, forcing later episodes to rediscover similar solutions. We study whether finalized repair outcomes can improve subsequent Text-to-SQL episodes without parameter updates. We introduce MERIT, a training-free agent that maintains an online dual-polarity memory of oracle-verified corrections and observed unsuccessful directions. Under oracle-assisted benchmark feedback, only memories from earlier finalized episodes are eligible for retrieval. A deterministic classifier assigns a coarse failure type, which conditions a hybrid lexical-dense retriever before the frozen model generates each revision. Using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with identical initial predictions and repair budgets, MERIT improves execution accuracy over stateless iterative repair from \(66.34\%\) to \(69.79\%\) on Spider and from \(47.35\%\) to \(48.44\%\) on BIRD. Paired analyses provide clear evidence for the Spider gain but weaker evidence on BIRD. MERIT is not reliably separated from untyped dynamic retrieval on either benchmark, while Reflexion-style memory reaches \(51.24\%\) on BIRD at substantially higher inference cost. Ablations show that negative memory contributes modestly, the value of type conditioning and lexical--dense ranking is dataset dependent, and schema-local experience provides the most consistent benefit. These results clarify when causal cross-query memory improves repair and when broader memory representations remain preferable.

</details>


### [157] [Clinical Communication Processing with Models Trained on LLM-Generated Synthetic Data: A Structured Survey and Novel Application Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05993)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了利用大型语言模型合成临床交流文本（如电子病历、诊断标签等转译为医患沟通语言）的方法，提出结构化叙事综述框架，并辅以13个无真实标注数据的临床NLP案例研究，证明合成通信可引导下游系统开发，但真实数据迁移验证仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床价值大量蕴含在交流语言中（如医患对话、交接班、急救报告），而非结构化记录。然而真实临床交流数据涉及隐私、碎片化且标注成本高昂，导致语料稀缺。大语言模型可通过将临床源数据转化为合成交流文本来解决这一瓶颈，但目前缺乏系统性梳理与实证评估。

Method: 采用结构化叙事综述方法，按四个维度组织文献：源表示、交流形式与参与者、生成方法、下游任务；并补充13个原创案例研究，涵盖EMS预抵达报告、野战电台伤员记录、护士交接班、患者门户分诊及低资源出院沟通等场景，在无真实标注数据的条件下构建临床NLP系统。

Result: 合成临床交流可引导相关NLP系统构建；微调编码器模型优于所评估的零样本基线；刻意劣化（degraded）的合成通信对系统鲁棒性有价值。主要局限是大多数研究仅在留出的合成数据上评估，训练于合成数据、测试于真实数据的证据仍然不足。

Conclusion: 合成临床交流正在成为一种实用的研究资源；若要将其确立为可复用的临床基础设施，仍需真实数据迁移验证、安全性评估和外部验证。

Abstract: Much clinical value is conveyed not through structured records but through communication: exchanges in which patients describe symptoms, clinicians reason and give instructions, ambulances hand over to emergency departments, and nurses pass on a shift. Such language differs from tabular data because meaning depends on speaker role, intent, causality, uncertainty, omission, and channel noise. Healthcare natural language processing must therefore interpret information as conveyed rather than coded. This requires well-annotated corpora, which are scarce because authentic exchanges are private, fragmented, and costly to annotate. Large language models offer a way forward by transforming clinical sources, such as records, diagnostic labels, symptom lists, or care plans, into written and transcribed communication for downstream models. We present a structured narrative survey organized by source representation, communication form and participants, generation method, and downstream task, complemented by thirteen novel case studies. These build clinical NLP systems for communication channels and languages without labeled real-world data, including EMS pre-arrival reports, field-radio casualty documentation, nurse handoffs, patient-portal triage, and low-resource discharge communication. They show that synthetic communication can bootstrap such systems. Findings include the competitiveness of fine-tuned encoder models over evaluated zero-shot baselines and the value of deliberately degraded communication for robustness. The main limitation is that most studies evaluate on held-out synthetic communication, while train-on-synthetic, test-on-authentic evidence remains limited. We conclude that syn-thetic clinical communication is becoming a practical research resource; establishing it as reusable clinical infrastructure will require authentic-data transfer, safety and external validation.

</details>


### [158] [EpiBench: Can LLMs Understand Epitopes for Antibody Drug Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06022)
*Zirui Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Qinghan Wang,Yuzhi Xu,Gang Du,Tingjun Hou,Odin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiBench，一个基于序列、闭卷、可自动评分的基准测试集，用于评估大语言模型在抗体表位推理方面的能力。该基准包含1,609个基于结构接触、功能性B细胞实验和深度突变扫描逃逸数据的样本，涵盖五个相互关联的任务。评估九个通用LLM后发现，当前模型仅能捕获部分表位相关信号，在抗体特异性序列 grounding、长上下文残基定位和生物学推理方面仍有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表位决定抗体与抗原结合的位置，影响功能性阻断和逃逸抗性等治疗特性，是抗体药物发现的核心。尽管LLM展现出强大的生物医学推理能力，但缺乏一个专门评估LLM能否直接从抗原和抗体序列推断表位信息的基准。现有表位资源要么聚焦孤立预测任务，要么依赖专门的结构设定，通用蛋白基准也未覆盖抗体开发流程中的表位中心决策。

Method: 作者构建了EpiBench基准，包含1,609个精选样本，数据来源于结构级抗体-抗原接触、功能性B细胞实验以及深度突变扫描逃逸测量。基准涵盖五个相互关联的任务：靶向区域发现、抗体条件性表位识别、表位分箱、功能性表位评估和抗体逃逸评估，并通过受控采样减少捷径式评估伪影。作者评估了九个通用LLM，并通过任务特定基线、抗原长度分层、显式推理比较和失败模式检查来分析模型行为。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前LLM能够捕获部分表位相关信号，但在抗体特异性序列grounding、长上下文残基定位以及生物学grounded推理方面仍存在明显局限，尚未达到可靠辅助抗体发现所需的水平。

Conclusion: EpiBench为衡量和提升序列感知型生物医学LLM提供了诊断测试平台，有助于推动LLM辅助抗体药物发现能力的发展，并揭示了现有模型在表位推理上的关键短板。

Abstract: Epitopes determine where antibodies bind antigens and shape downstream therapeutic properties such as functional blockade and escape resistance, making epitope understanding central to antibody drug discovery. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong biomedical reasoning ability, it remains unclear whether they can infer epitope information directly from antigen and antibody sequences. Existing epitope resources typically focus on isolated prediction tasks or rely on specialized structural settings, while general protein benchmarks do not evaluate epitope-centered decisions across the antibody development workflow. To address this gap, we introduce EpiBench, a closed-book, sequence-based, and automatically scorable benchmark for evaluating epitope reasoning in LLMs. EpiBench contains 1,609 curated samples grounded in structural antibody--antigen contacts, curated functional B-cell assays, and deep mutational scanning escape measurements. It covers five connected tasks: targetable region discovery, antibody-conditioned epitope identification, epitope binning, functional epitope assessment, and antibody escape assessment, with controlled sampling to reduce shortcut-based evaluation artifacts. We evaluate nine general-purpose LLMs and analyze their behavior through task-specific baselines, antigen length stratification, explicit-reasoning comparison, and failure-mode inspection. The results show that current LLMs capture partial epitope-related signals but remain limited in antibody-specific sequence grounding, long-context residue localization, and biologically grounded reasoning. Therefore, EpiBench provides a diagnostic testbed for measuring and improving sequence-aware biomedical LLMs toward reliable LLM-assisted antibody discovery.

</details>


### [159] [FormBharo: Designing and Evaluating a Voice Agent for Conversational Form Filling in Rural India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06027)
*Aman Dalmia,Sanskriti Midha,Jigar Doshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍FormBharo，一个为印度低识字率人群设计的语音代理系统，通过电话对话自动填写结构化表格。该系统结合LLM与确定性规则校验，已在ARMMAN NGO试点用于低收入的印地语母亲产前产后护理注册。作者还发布了FormVoiceAgentBench基准（3,760个多轮对话测试、960次模拟通话），发现真实语音转录错误会使表格完成率下降41个百分点，规则控制可帮助小模型媲美甚至超越前沿模型，且组件性能不能预测端到端性能，最终需通过Pareto加权标量法在准确性、成本和延迟间权衡选择部署配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度几乎所有社会福利都始于表格填写，但最需要这些福利的人群往往不识字，需要面对面的口语交流来完成注册。当前由前线卫生工作者一对一地逐一登记受益人，效率低下且浪费有限的人力资源。作者希望通过语音代理自动化这一流程，为低识字率的弱势人群提供可及的福利获取途径。

Method: 构建FormBharo语音代理系统，采用LLM与确定性规则验证和流程控制相结合的方式，在电话通话中实时填写结构化表格。同时开发FormVoiceAgentBench基准，使用人工录制的印地语音频配合3,760个多轮对话测试和960次模拟通话，评估转录、信息抽取、回复生成等组件及端到端表格完成效果。最后使用基于Pareto的加权标量法在准确率、成本和延迟三个维度上进行多目标优化选型。

Result: 真实语音转录相比参考转录使表格完成率下降最多约41个百分点。规则控制能恢复许多轮次级别的抽取错误，使更小更便宜的模型在表格完成率上匹配甚至超越前沿模型。组件性能无法预测端到端性能——GPT-5.5在参考转录上的轮次抽取准确率达99.8%但在表格完成率上排名较低。没有单一模型能同时在准确率、成本和延迟上最优，需通过Pareto加权标量法选择折中的部署配置。

Conclusion: 语音代理可以帮助低识字率人群完成社会福利表格填写，但端到端评估至关重要，因为错误在流水线中既会传播也会相互抵消，仅评估组件性能会产生误导。规则控制可以有效弥补LLM在真实语音转录下的不足，且部署选型必须综合考虑准确率、成本和延迟等多维目标。

Abstract: In India, almost every social benefit starts with a form, yet the people who need these benefits most are often unable to read or write. Reaching them requires a spoken conversation. Today that work falls to frontline health workers who enroll beneficiaries one at a time, a poor use of stretched capacity. We built FormBharo ("fill the form" in Hindi), a voice agent that fills a structured form over a phone call under tight latency and cost budgets by pairing Large Language Models (LLMs) with deterministic, rule-based validation and flow control. It is being piloted with ARMMAN, an NGO running large-scale maternal and child mobile-health programs in India, to enroll low-income, Hindi-speaking mothers in antenatal and postnatal care. To our knowledge, it is the first voice agent piloted to fill an enrollment form for this population. We openly release FormVoiceAgentBench, a benchmark pairing human-recorded Hindi audio with 3,760 multi-turn conversation tests across 960 simulated calls, to evaluate our agent's components (transcription, extraction, reply generation) and end-to-end form completion under real acoustic variations. Form completion drops by up to ~41 points when LLMs receive error-prone real-speech transcripts instead of reference ones. The rule-based controls recover many turn-level extraction errors, helping smaller, cheaper models match or surpass frontier models on form completion. Component performance does not predict end-to-end performance: GPT-5.5 leads turn-level extraction accuracy on reference transcripts (99.8%) but ranks lower on form completion. Since errors both propagate and cancel across the pipeline, the optimal model choice of models emerges only through end-to-end evaluation. Finally, no single model is best across accuracy, cost, and latency at once, so we use a Pareto-based weighted-sum scalarization to select a deployable configuration balancing the three.

</details>


### [160] [Training-Free Token-Level Steering for LLM Personalized Co-Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06069)
*Wenhao Mao,Chengbin Hou,Weixiao Wang,Jialiang Zhu,Min Liu,Yibin Hao,Hairong Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SteerWrite，一个无需训练的个性化协同写作框架，通过无梯度更新的方式使基础大语言模型适配专业领域，在小数据集上实现SOTA性能并减少人工编辑成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在个性化任务中缺乏专业领域知识；传统微调方法计算成本高且难以应对快速数据更新；检索增强生成（RAG）无法提供细粒度的token级控制；现有交互范式多为聊天式，缺乏超越编程领域的协同写作范式。

Method: 提出SteerWrite，一个无需训练（training-free）的框架，通过特定设计适配小数据集，在不进行梯度更新的情况下将基础模型引导至专业领域，实现token级的个性化写作控制。

Result: 在多种数据集、指标和模型上均达到SOTA性能，显著减少了人工编辑工作量。

Conclusion: SteerWrite证明了无需梯度更新的训练-free框架可以有效实现个性化协同写作，兼顾领域适配性、细粒度控制和小数据场景的实用性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for personalization, they often lack specialized domain knowledge. Conventional solutions like fine-tuning struggle with high computational costs and rapid data updates, while Retrieval-Augmented Generation fails to provide fine-grained, token-level steering. Furthermore, chat-based interfaces remain dominant, whereas productive co-writing paradigms have not yet been well exploited beyond the coding domain. To this end, we introduce SteerWrite, a training-free framework designed for personalized co-writing. Our method effectively adapts the base model to specialized domains without gradient updates, with specific designs tailored to small datasets. Experiments demonstrate that SteerWrite achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse datasets, metrics, and models, significantly reducing human editing effort.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Sequence Order: Syntax-Informed Positional Embeddings for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06111)
*Haris Riaz,Hyungji Kim,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语法感知位置嵌入（SiPE），在预训练阶段从依存句法解析中学习轻量级语法先验，并注入到绝对、相对、旋转三种主流位置嵌入家族中。SiPE在SyntaxGym基准上提升高达10.3%，同时困惑度降低9.0%，GLUE基准提升8.2%，且推理时仅需单个句法解析，建立了语法监督与推理成本之间的新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的位置嵌入主要编码token距离和顺序，但对句法结构基本不敏感。现有语法注入方法通常在推理时边际化多个解析或直接丢弃句法信息，且多数方法会损害困惑度等指标。作者希望设计一种轻量级、成本可控的语法感知位置嵌入方案。

Method: SiPE在预训练阶段从依存句法解析中学习轻量级语法先验，并将其注入三种主流PE家族（绝对、相对、旋转）中，适用于编码器和解码器。研究发现注入位置取决于架构：自回归解码器（使用相对PE）中，语法先验与注意力分数的相对位置项乘法耦合效果最佳；编码器中，直接加至输入嵌入效果最好。模型保持自注意力和其余架构不变。

Result: 与无语法监督的基线模型相比，SiPE在SyntaxGym上提升高达10.3%，困惑度降低9.0%（多数现有语法注入方法反而会恶化困惑度）。在真实语言理解任务上，GLUE基准提升8.2%。SiPE仅需推理时单个解析，优于需要边际化多个解析的方法。

Conclusion: SiPE证明了语法先验在位置嵌入中的有效性与通用性，适用于多种PE家族和架构类型。它同时改善了语法泛化能力和真实语言理解能力，且不增加推理成本，在语法监督与推理效率之间建立了新的帕累托最优。

Abstract: Positional embeddings (PE) in Transformers encode token distance and order but are largely agnostic to \textit{syntactic structure}. We introduce \textbf{S}yntax-\textbf{i}nformed \textbf{P}ositional \textbf{E}mbeddings (\textbf{SiPE}), which learns a lightweight syntactic prior from dependency parses during pretraining and injects it across all three dominant PE families (absolute, relative, rotary), for both encoders and decoders, leaving self-attention and the rest of the architecture untouched. We isolate \emph{where} and \emph{how} the prior should enter the model, and find it depends on the architecture: for autoregressive decoders that use relative PE, the prior is strongest when coupled multiplicatively with the relative-position term of the attention score, outperforming injection into the input embeddings, into self-attention, or into the positional and attention terms jointly---while for encoders it is best added directly to the input embeddings, composing with each encoder's native positional mechanism. We find that models pre-trained with SiPE improve on the SyntaxGym benchmark by up to $10.3\%$ while simultaneously reducing perplexity by $9.0\%$ over a base model with no syntactic supervision---a metric nearly every existing syntax-injection method instead degrades. Crucially, these gains extend beyond syntactic generalization: SiPE also improves real-world language understanding, raising scores on the GLUE benchmark by up to $8.2\%$ over a model trained without it. Unlike existing syntactic language models that marginalize over many parses at inference or discard syntax at runtime, SiPE conditions on a single parse, establishing a new Pareto frontier between syntactic supervision and inference cost.

</details>


### [162] [Decolonizing Linguistic Policies in Automated Speech Recognition: A Framework for Cross-Culturally Competent Speech AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06141)
*Jay L. Cunningham,Mark Atta Mensah,Richard Martinez,Joao Vieira da Silva Neto,Efi Dawodu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨自动语音识别（ASR）及其语音界面在公共服务、医疗和教育领域的影响，指出ASR对低资源、原住民和非标准语言变体的持续失败不仅是技术缺陷，更是隐含的语言政策，反映了殖民语言等级制度。论文提出三危害（3M）分类法和七层情境化模型，并倡导参与式框架和最低审计协议，以促进文化胜任的ASR系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统在低资源语言、原住民语言和非标准语言变体上的持续失败被错误地视为单纯的技术问题，而实际上这些失败隐含着语言政策的维度，延续了殖民时代的语言等级体系。作者希望揭示ASR技术背后的权力结构，并提出相应的解决方案。

Method: 论文综合运用语言资本、种族语言学意识形态、语言政策研究和去殖民计算等理论视角进行分析，提出了三危害（3M）分类法——包括误识别、误对齐和不信任——以及面向ASR语言多样性的七层情境化模型，并设计了参与式框架和最低审计协议。

Result: 提出了系统性的理论框架：3M分类法用于识别ASR系统中的三类危害（误识别、误对齐、不信任），七层情境化模型用于分析ASR中的语言多样性问题，同时构建了参与式设计框架，将受影响社区定位为共同设计者、评估者和治理伙伴。

Conclusion: 解决ASR系统中的语言不平等问题需要超越纯技术层面的修复，必须将受影响社区纳入ASR的设计、评估和治理全过程，并建立文化胜任的审计机制，以实现更具包容性和公平性的语音技术。

Abstract: This paper focuses on automatic speech recognition (ASR) and ASR-mediated voice interfaces that shape access to public services, healthcare, and education. We argue that persistent failures for low-resource, Indigenous, and non-standard language varieties are not only technical errors, but also implicit linguistic policies that reproduce colonial language hierarchies. Drawing on linguistic capital, raciolinguistic ideology, language policy research, and decolonial computing, we show how data, metrics, and model priors determine whose voices become machine-legible. We introduce the Three Harms (3M) taxonomy---Misrecognition, Misalignment, and Mistrust---and a seven-layer situatedness model for linguistic diversity in ASR and ASR-mediated voice interfaces. We then propose a participatory framework and minimum audit protocol for culturally competent ASR, positioning affected communities as co-designers, evaluators, and governance partners.

</details>


### [163] [Routing Is Least Learnable Where It Is Most Valuable: Bounds on Representation Routing for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06171)
*Jiaming Wei,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama,Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了Web代理在六种观察模式下的性能（文本、像素或混合），涵盖8个站点-模型组合。发现不同模式具有互补性，但"预言机"选择带来的收益被运行噪声（12-14%结果变化）大幅夸大。唯一稳健的收益是成本约束：只把无模式能解决的任务发送给最廉价模式，可在8/8场景中降低成本9.5-30.6%且成功率不变。五种路由策略均无法稳健地超越固定选择一个好模式。核心障碍在于路由监督标签受限于代理自身成功率，但标签供给与路由机会高度相关（0.95），更强代理可能扭转结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web代理通常为所有任务固定一种观察模式（文本、像素或组合），但不同模式在不同任务上表现各异。作者想探究：如果根据任务动态选择观察模式，能带来多大收益？这种"路由"策略是否值得投入？

Method: 在VisualWebArena和WebArena上，对8个站点-模型组合测量6种观察模式；通过重复运行量化噪声；评估预言机路由的上界收益；提出并测试5种路由策略（模式选择、强模式投入时机、基于任务文本的零成本规则、置信度级联、成本分层）；分析路由监督标签与代理成功率的关系。

Result: 1) 六种模式互补，最优选择在任务集间反转；2) 预言机收益被12-14%的运行噪声夸大，重跑已有模式与新增模式的增益相当；3) 仅成本约束（将无解任务发给最廉价模式）在全部8/8场景中降低成本9.5-30.6%且不改成功率；4) 五种路由策略均无法稳健胜过固定模式，仅一个稀疏场景出现脆弱例外；5) 路由监督标签供给与路由机会相关性达0.95，弱代理限制了路由价值。

Conclusion: 当前的Web代理水平下，任务级观察模式路由并不能带来稳健的收益，固定选择一种好模式仍是可靠的默认方案。但这一限制源于当前代理能力不足而非路由本身无效——随着代理变强，标签供给增加，路由的价值有望显现。作者提供了完整的测量协议和噪声带以供后续研究复现。

Abstract: Web agents observe a browser through text, pixels, or both, and the choice is usually fixed once for all tasks. We measure six observation modes across eight site-model combinations (cells) on VisualWebArena and WebArena and ask what choosing per task would buy. The modes are complementary: each solves tasks the others miss, they fail in structurally different ways, and the best choice reverses between task sets. The obvious prize, an oracle that picks a winning mode for every task, looks large but is inflated by run-to-run noise: rerunning the same mode on the same tasks changes 12-14% of outcomes, so a second run of a mode already in hand gains about as much as adding a new one. What survives is a cost bound: sending only the tasks no mode solves to the cheapest mode cuts cost by 9.5-30.6% in 8 of 8 cells at unchanged success. We then test five routing policies (picking the mode, deciding when to spend on the strong mode, a zero-cost rule read off the task text, a confidence cascade, and pooled cost tiers), and none robustly beats simply fixing one well-chosen mode; the one exception is a fragile result in our sparsest cell. The central obstruction is that routing supervision is produced at the agent's success rate: the weaker the agent, the fewer labels a router gets, exactly where routing would be most valuable. This limit belongs to today's agents rather than to routing itself. Label supply and routing opportunity rise together (correlation 0.95 across cells), so a stronger agent can overturn the result, and we report the rerun noise bands and the full measurement protocol.

</details>


### [164] [NeSy-RAG: Neuro-Symbolic RAG for Explainable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06292)
*Jonas Gann,Michael Gertz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NeSy-RAG是一个模块化神经符号RAG框架，通过从文本块合成可归属的Prolog模块，将LLM推理转化为确定性的符号执行，并结合知识缺口检测机制，在ShARC基准上达到61.1%准确率，远超同模型RAG基线（42.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的推理过程不透明，中间推理步骤难以验证和归属到具体证据；同时，用户特定上下文的缺失很少被系统检测，常导致不完整或错误的输出。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 从检索的文本块合成可归属的Prolog模块，生成语义谓词编码布尔声明；(2) 使用联合自然语言-代码嵌入进行谓词检索和组合成Prolog查询；(3) 引入符号知识缺口检测机制，识别影响查询结果的缺失用户事实并自动触发后续交互。

Result: 在ShARC基准上，无需领域特定训练，NeSy-RAG达到61.1%准确率，优于同模型RAG基线（42.8%），大幅提升约18个百分点。

Conclusion: NeSy-RAG通过符号化推理和知识缺口检测，实现了透明、可归属且能主动发现缺失上下文的RAG框架，验证了神经符号方法在提升RAG可解释性和准确率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves question answering by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge such as text corpora. However, its reasoning process remains largely opaque: intermediate reasoning steps are difficult to verify and cannot be reliably attributed to specific evidence. Moreover, missing user-specific context is rarely detected systematically, often leading to incomplete or incorrect output.
  We propose NeSy-RAG, a modular neuro-symbolic RAG framework that synthesizes attributable Prolog modules from retrieved text chunks. For each chunk, the system generates semantically meaningful predicates that encode Boolean claims, which may depend on user facts. Using joint natural language-code embeddings, predicates are retrieved and composed into Prolog queries. To address incomplete user context, we introduce a symbolic knowledge-gap detection mechanism that identifies missing user facts whose truth values affect the query outcome and automatically triggers follow-up interactions.
  Executing the resulting Prolog queries yields deterministic answers together with transparent execution traces that link each reasoning step to its originating source. On the ShARC benchmark, without domain-specific training, NeSy-RAG achieves 61.1% accuracy, outperforming a same-model RAG baseline that achieves 42.8% accuracy.

</details>


### [165] [Benchmarking and Enhancing LLMs for Rule-Intensive Review of National Standard Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06312)
*Tao Wang,Qihao Yang,Rongjiao Liang,Lianghong Lin,Haitao Wang,Xinyu Cao,Tianyong Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GB/T-Bench，这是首个面向国家标准文档结构化审查的基准，包含一个层次化的审查分类体系（涵盖5个维度、25种可诊断错误类型），通过可控反例生成机制处理488份文档生成7,306个可追溯的审查错误实例，并提出了GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架。实验表明最强LLM仅达到0.3280 CMCS（专家为0.6640），而GB/T-Reviewer将最佳成绩提升至0.5094，揭示了人机差距并展示了结构化技能协作的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在规则密集型的专业文档审查任务中的能力尚未得到充分评估。现有基准主要关注领域知识和问答，忽略了专业人员文档的内在质量审查这一重要任务。国家标准文档（如中国GB/T标准）提供了一个理想的测试场景——它们篇幅长、结构高度规范、受明确的规则约束，但审查高度依赖人类专家，成本高且难以规模化。

Method: (1) 构建GB/T Review Taxonomy层次化审查分类体系，涵盖文档结构、范围一致性、规范性模态、术语一致性和规范性引用5大维度，包含25种可诊断错误类型；(2) 设计可控反例生成机制，结合确定性规则和受约束的LLM改写，将488份文档处理为7,306个可追溯的错误实例；(3) 提出诊断导向的评估协议，要求在错误位置、审查维度和错误类型上精确匹配，并加入文档级覆盖率指标；(4) 提出GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架，将审查知识转化为专业技能，协调全局检查、定向诊断、规则扫描和结果验证四个环节。

Result: 对14个主流LLM的实验显示，最强模型仅达到0.3280的CMCS分数，而人类专家达到0.6640，存在显著的人机差距。GB/T-Reviewer框架将最佳CMCS提升至0.5094，显著缩小了与专家的差距，验证了结构化技能协调对规则密集型文档审查的有效性。

Conclusion: 这项工作填补了专业文档内在质量审查基准的空白，展示了多智能体技能协作在规则密集型审查任务中的潜力，为标准制定及其他高风险文档领域可信AI的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support complex professional tasks, yet their capabilities in rule-intensive document review remain insufficiently evaluated. National standard documents, such as China GB/T standards, offer a representative testbed: they are lengthy, highly structured, and governed by explicit rules for scope, terminology, normative wording, and cross-section consistency. Existing benchmarks focus on domain knowledge and question answering, largely overlooking intrinsic quality review for professional documents. Such reviews rely heavily on human experts, making them costly and difficult to scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce GB/T-Bench, the first benchmark for the structured review of national standard documents. Its GB/T Review Taxonomy is a hierarchical schema covering document structure, scope alignment, normative modality, terminology consistency, and normative references, with 25 diagnosable error types. A controllable counterexample generation mechanism combines deterministic rules and constrained LLM rewriting to process 488 documents into 7,306 traceable review error instances for evaluation. We also develop a diagnosis-oriented evaluation protocol requiring exact matches on error location, review dimension, and error type, plus document-level coverage metrics. We further propose GB/T-Reviewer, a multi-agent framework that converts review knowledge into specialized skills and coordinates global inspection, targeted diagnosis, rule scanning, and result verification. Experiments with 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a substantial human-LLM gap: the strongest model achieves only 0.3280 CMCS versus 0.6640 for experts. GB/T-Reviewer raises the best CMCS to 0.5094, showing the value of structured skill coordination for rule-intensive document review. This work paves the way for trustworthy AI in standardization and other high-stakes document domains.

</details>


### [166] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Benchmarks for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06329)
*Noam Koren,Roy Bar-Haim,Abigail Goldsteen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考标注的框架，利用LLM评判员评估任务型对话智能体基准测试的质量，包括一致性、复杂度和策略覆盖度三个维度，并提供可操作的诊断信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务型对话智能体的评估依赖基准测试，但基准测试本身的质量很少被评估。低质量的基准测试可能包含不一致的任务、过于简单的场景或有限的策略覆盖，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 提出一个无参考（reference-free）框架，使用LLM评判员从三个维度评估基准测试质量：一致性（consistency）、复杂度（complexity）和策略覆盖度（policy coverage），并提供可操作的弱点诊断。通过人工标注一致性验证和多种质量扰动实验进行验证。

Result: 在多个领域和不同评判模型下，所提出的指标能够一致地区分不同质量的基准测试。框架还适用于人工整理的基准测试。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估合成和人工整理的对话智能体基准测试提供了一种实用的方法，能够有效识别基准质量差异并给出诊断性反馈。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents are evaluated using curated or automatically generated benchmarks, yet benchmark quality is rarely assessed. Poor benchmarks may contain inconsistent tasks, simplistic scenarios, or limited policy coverage, leading to unreliable evaluations. We introduce a reference-free framework that uses LLM judges to assess benchmark consistency, complexity, and policy coverage, while providing actionable diagnostics of weaknesses. We validate the framework by demonstrating agreement with independent human annotations and by evaluating benchmarks generated by LLMs of varying capabilities, as well as benchmarks subjected to controlled quality-degrading perturbations. Across domains and judge models, the proposed metrics consistently distinguish between benchmark quality levels. We further demonstrate the framework's applicability to manually curated benchmarks. Our framework offers a practical approach for evaluating synthetic and manually curated conversational-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [167] [RP-OPSD: Reasoning-Pivot-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06347)
*Xinye Wang,Junxiao Liu,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RP-OPSD方法，通过在生成推理过程中识别"推理枢纽"（reasoning pivots）并引导特权蒸馏，增强LLM的多语言推理迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言推理迁移对将LLM的推理能力扩展到非高资源语言至关重要。现有on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）方法虽提供密集token级监督，但其目标未显式优先处理对跨语言迁移最关键的推理信号。作者观察到目标语言的推理包含表层文本生成和推理枢纽（推进或改变推理方向、塑造后续推断的决策），需要围绕这些枢纽进行特权蒸馏。

Method: 提出RP-OPSD（Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation），利用匹配的教师视图（有无英文参考解答）之间的分布偏移作为操作代理，以引导特权蒸馏和参考锚定，从而将训练集中在推理枢纽上。

Result: 在涵盖17种语言和多个难度级别的数学推理基准上，RP-OPSD优于强多语言推理基线和OPSD变体。进一步分析表明，该方法将特权蒸馏集中在推理控制和问题条件状态更新token上，同时降低对主要支持表层实现的token的权重。

Conclusion: 通过识别和聚焦推理枢纽而非表层文本，可以有效提升多语言推理迁移性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning transfer is crucial for extending reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) beyond high-resource languages. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) and its variants have emerged as a promising paradigm, providing dense token-level supervision on student-generated rollouts, yet their objectives do not explicitly prioritize reasoning signals most critical to cross-lingual transfer. We characterize that target-language reasoning comprises the generation of both surface text and reasoning pivots, which are decisions that advance or redirect the reasoning process and shape subsequent inference. This motivates concentrating privileged distillation around such pivots. We therefore propose RP-OPSD, Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, using the distributional shift between matched teacher views with and without an English reference solution as an operational proxy to guide privileged distillation and reference anchoring. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks covering 17 languages and multiple difficulty levels show that our method outperforms strong multilingual reasoning baselines and OPSD variants. Further analysis reveals that RP-OPSD concentrates privileged distillation on reasoning-control and problem-condistioned state-update tokens, while downweighting it for tokens that mainly support surface realization. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/RP-OPSD.

</details>


### [168] [The Bitter Lesson of Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06370)
*Ishan Patel,Sahil Sen,Elias Lumer,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了程序化工具调用（PTC）与原生的JSON工具调用在14个语言模型上的表现（基于BFCL v4基准）。结果表明，PTC在11/14的模型中达到或超越JSON基线，GPT-5.6系列提升10.6%，在并行扇出场景下13/14模型表现更优，且在上下文退化条件下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏在既定基准上、跨当前及前代模型、在真实任务条件下对"工具即代码"（程序化工具调用）进行系统评估的工作。作者希望填补这一空白，系统比较程序化工具调用与原生JSON工具调用的性能差异。

Method: 在BFCL v4基准上，对14个语言模型进行程序化工具调用（PTC）与原生JSON工具调用的实证对比。PTC范式将工具暴露为类型化的Python桩（stub），模型通过代码调用工具，执行与结果处理在单次代理回合内完成。评估覆盖并行扇出和上下文退化等多种任务条件。

Result: 程序化工具调用在14个模型中11个达到或超越JSON工具调用基线；GPT-5.6系列提升10.6%；并行扇出场景下13/14个模型匹配或超越基线；在上下文退化条件下保持稳定（基线平均退化2.3%）。

Conclusion: 程序化工具调用是JSON工具调用的可行且稳健的替代方案，其性能随模型发布代际的能力提升而同步提升，展示了该范式的广泛适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Tool use transforms LLMs into agents that act beyond their training data, and for code-capable models, programmatic tool calling extends this further by replacing rigid JSON calls with scripts that chain and parallelize naturally. However, a systematic evaluation of tools as code on an established benchmark across current and prior model generations under real-world task conditions has not been conducted. In this work, we empirically compare programmatic tool calling (PTC) to native JSON tool calling across 14 language models on BFCL v4. In the programmatic tool calling paradigm, tools are exposed as typed Python stubs that the model invokes through code, with execution and results handled in a single agent turn. Programmatic tool calling matches or exceeds native JSON tool calling in 11 of 14 models on BFCL v4, with the GPT-5.6 family achieving a 10.6% improvement over the JSON tool calling baseline. Further, it matches or outperforms baseline in 13 of 14 models under parallel fan-out, and holds stable under context rot conditions where baseline degrades 2.3% on average. Our results demonstrate that programmatic tool calling is a viable and robust alternative to JSON tool calling, with performance tracking model capability across release generations.

</details>


### [169] [Learning When to Trust via Selective Context Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06377)
*Xian Sun,Wei Chow,Yingshuo Wang,Junhao Liu,Wei Gao,Qing Wu,Lingdong Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言模型对上下文信号的依赖问题重新定义为"选择性信任"问题，引入MIST基准（四种匹配条件下的推理任务）和SC2W配对指标，并提出SCOPE方法来优化模型的选择性信任能力，在降低误导性信号危害的同时保持对有效上下文的利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型依赖外部信号生成答案，但单个误导性信号可能将正确答案变错。简单的"抵抗诱惑"训练方案存在缺陷：完全忽略上下文的模型看似鲁棒，却无法利用有价值的上下文信息。因此需要将问题从"抵抗"重构为"选择性信任"。

Method: 1) 引入MIST基准：每个人类标注的推理项目在四种匹配条件下呈现（干净、误导、正确上下文、无关上下文）；2) 提出SC2W配对指标：衡量误导信号将干净正确答案翻转错误的频率；3) 提出SCOPE方法：挖掘干净正确/误导错误的失败样本，对四种条件均衡配对的偏好对优化标准DPO目标，而非仅针对误导样本。

Result: 在全面的基准研究中，观察到误导信号引起的答案翻转具有普遍性。SCOPE方法在流行的开源模型上大幅降低了SC2W指标，同时保持了在干净、正确或无关上下文条件下的准确性。

Conclusion: 模型应以"选择性信任"能力来评判，而非仅仅以"抵抗"能力来评判——即模型既不应盲目信任上下文信号，也不应完全忽视有价值的上下文。

Abstract: Language models increasingly condition their answers on external signals, and a single misleading one can turn a correct answer wrong. The obvious remedy, training models to resist such signals, hides a failure mode: a model that ignores all context looks robust yet is useless when the context is worth trusting. We recast the problem as selective trust and introduce MIST, a human-annotated benchmark that renders each reasoning item under four matched conditions (clean, misleading, correct-context, and irrelevant-context), together with SC2W, a paired metric counting how often a misleading signal flips a clean-correct answer to wrong. Across a comprehensive benchmark study, we observe that such a susceptibility is universal. We then propose SCOPE, which mines clean-correct/misleading-wrong failures and optimizes a standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) objective over matched preference pairs balanced equally across all four conditions, rather than over misleading items alone. Our approach substantially reduces SC2W on popular open-sourced models while preserving accuracy when the added context is clean, correct, or irrelevant. With this work, we argue that models should be judged on selective trust, not on resistance alone.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Cross-Sectional Heterogeneity in LSTM Networks for Financial Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05755)
*Julius Döbelt*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的LSTM架构，通过集成宏观金融协变量和可学习的行业嵌入（sector embeddings）来预测S&P 500成分股的日度收益方向，并基于此构建多空投资组合。实验证明该模型在风险和收益指标上优于基础LSTM、随机森林和市场买入持有策略，同时通过潜在空间可视化和权重贡献度量提升了模型可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融资产收益预测面临低信噪比和半强式市场有效性的挑战。标准深度学习模型（尤其是LSTM）虽然能捕捉时间依赖性，但往往无法处理资产收益的横截面异质性（cross-sectional heterogeneity），即不同行业/板块的收益特征差异。

Method: 在基础LSTM架构上进行扩展：1) 集成宏观金融协变量捕捉更广泛的经济信号；2) 引入可学习的行业嵌入（learnable sector embeddings）以纳入行业异质性。交易策略为基于S&P 500各成分股日度方向预测构建多空组合，判断个股相对横截面中位数收益的超额或落后表现。此外，通过潜在空间可视化分析模型对行业的区分能力，并提出一种新颖的贡献度量方法来量化行业信息在LSTM中的影响。

Result: 实证结果表明，带行业嵌入的LSTM模型在关键风险和收益指标上优于所有基准（基础LSTM、随机森林、市场买入持有策略）。预测信号主要由短期反转因子和行业动量因子驱动。

Conclusion: 行业嵌入能够显式地将横截面异质性纳入深度学习模型，使其适应市场中不同行业动态，从而提高预测准确性和可解释性。该框架为深度学习在金融预测中的应用提供了一种兼顾性能与透明度的可行方案。

Abstract: Predicting financial asset returns remains one of the most difficult challenges in empirical finance, driven by the low signal-to-noise ratio and the semi-strong form of market efficiency. While deep learning models, especially LSTM networks, have shown promise in capturing temporal dependencies, standard architectures often struggle to account for the cross-sectional heterogeneity of asset returns. This paper proposes a novel architectural extension to the basic LSTM model designed to improve both predictive accuracy and model interpretability. The framework integrates macro-financial covariates to capture broader economic signals and learnable sector embeddings to encompass heterogeneity by sector. The trading strategy involves constructing a long-short portfolio based on daily directional forecasts for each S&P 500 constituent, targeting stocks expected to under- or outperform the cross-sectional median return of the S&P 500. Model Performance is evaluated against three competitive benchmarks: a basic LSTM, a Random Forest model and a traditional market buy-and-hold strategy. The empirical results demonstrate that the LSTM with sector embeddings outperforms all benchmarks across key risk and return metrics. By utilizing sector embeddings, the model explicitly incorporates cross-sectional heterogeneity, allowing it to adapt to varying industry dynamics within the market. To address the black-box nature of deep learning, I use latent space visualizations to analyse how the model differentiates between sectors, providing insights into the internal representation of the sectors in the LSTM. The impact of the sector information can be quantified using a novel contribution metric by inspecting the weights of the LSTM. The predictive signal is driven by a short-term reversal factor and an industry momentum factor.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [A Foundational EDM2-Based Generative Model for High-Resolution Synthetic Fetal Ultrasound Imaging from Open Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05471)
*Harvey Mannering,Yilin Zhang,Ziao Liu,Zhiwu Huang,Jacqueline Matthew,Miguel Xochicale*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于EDM2扩散架构的高分辨率胎儿超声图像合成框架，在多个公共数据集上训练，可生成512×512的六类解剖图像。该方法在FID评分上优于现有方法，并通过微调将下游胎儿平面分类准确率提升至93.36%，超越仅用真实数据训练的效果。尽管临床专家评价合成图像在真实感上仍低于真实图像（平均2.67/5分），但整体验证了合成数据在胎儿超声AI中的可行性和价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前超声成像对评估胎儿健康至关重要，但AI在该领域的发展受限于数据稀缺、隐私限制和标注困难等问题。现有真实数据集难以支持大规模深度学习模型的训练，因此需要合成数据来缓解数据瓶颈。

Method: 基于EDM2扩散架构构建高分辨率胎儿超声合成框架，在多个公共数据集上训练，生成512×512分辨率、涵盖六个解剖类别的超声图像。随后在下游任务中对模型进行微调用于胎儿平面分类，并通过临床专家评分进行质量评估。

Result: 该方法在FID评分上取得更低的图像质量指标；微调后胎儿平面分类的集成准确率达93.36%，优于仅使用真实数据训练的结果；临床专家对100张图像的真实感评分为2.67/5（真实图像评分更高），合成图像存在平滑、斑点不规则和解剖不一致等伪影。

Conclusion: 基于EDM2的合成框架能够生成高质量的胎儿超声图像，并在下游分类任务中验证了合成数据的有效性，证明合成数据可以作为真实数据的补充来提升AI模型性能。同时，合成图像在临床真实感方面仍有改进空间，未来需要进一步解决伪影问题。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound imaging is key for assessing fetal health, but AI progress is limited by scarce, privacy-restricted, and hard-to-annotate datasets. We propose a high-resolution fetal ultrasound synthesis framework based on the EDM2 diffusion architecture, trained on multiple public datasets to generate 512x512 images across six anatomical classes. Our method achieved improved image quality with lower FID scores and enhanced downstream fetal plane classification, reaching 93.36% ensemble accuracy after fine-tuning, surpassing real-data-only training. Clinical evaluation by an experienced fetal ultrasound specialist (10+ years) on 100 images yielded a mean realism score of 2.67/5, with real images rated higher than synthetic. Artefacts included smoothing, speckle irregularities, and anatomical inconsistencies. Code, data, models and other resources to reproduce this work are available at https://github.com/xfetus/fetal-ultrasound-edm2.

</details>


### [172] [Hyperspectral Calibration Detection: A Novel Concept For Change Detection With Unsupervised Incremental Safe Pseudo-Labeling Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06028)
*Chia-Hsiang Lin,Shih-Min Hsu,Ching-Yun Liang,Jocelyn Chanussot,Jhih-Yan Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全无监督的高光谱变化检测算法HyperLUCID，无需任何标签即可通过迭代增广训练集学习光谱校准函数，补偿双时相图像采集条件差异，实现高效且高精度的变化检测，适用于机载边缘计算场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准HCD算法多为半监督方法，虽可达到极低样本标注率，但在机载边缘计算等实际场景中，新获取图像的标注不可得，需要实现零标签要求。因此需要开发全无监督的HCD算法。

Method: 提出HyperLUCID算法：(1) 迭代增广训练集，安全收集未变化像素样本；(2) 学习迭代精化的光谱校准函数，补偿双时相图像的采集条件变异；(3) 通过校准后的光谱分析轻松检测变化像素；(4) 配套轻量级模型，适合机载检测任务。

Result: 该算法计算效率极高，比大多数基准HCD方法快约1到2个数量级；在多个真实基准HCD数据集上达到了93.6%至97.9%的总体精度，实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: HyperLUCID作为全无监督的HCD算法，在无需任何标签的前提下兼顾了计算效率和检测精度，为机载边缘计算等零标注场景提供了可行的解决方案，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Hyperspectral change detection (HCD) has found numerous key applications, such as land cover monitoring. The majority of benchmark HCD algorithms are semi-supervised methods, and some of them can even achieve very low sample labeling rates. However, in some practical scenarios, such as those requiring immediate detection responses for onboard edge computing, we need to achieve the zero-label requirement as ground-truth labeling would not be available onboard for newly acquired images. In this work, we propose a fully unsupervised HCD algorithm, together with a lightweight model, quite suitable for onboard detection missions. Based on an iteratively augmented training set that safely collects some unchanged pixel samples, we learn an iteratively refined spectrum calibration function that eventually compensates the variability of acquisition conditions (often observed in bitemporal images), thereby making the changed pixels easily detectable by analyzing the calibrated spectra. The proposed hyperspectral looping unsupervised calibration and incremental detection (HyperLUCID) algorithm is not only computationally efficient (around 1 to 2 orders of magnitude faster than most benchmark HCD methods), but has also achieved state-of-the-art results (around 93.6% to 97.9% overall accuracy) on several real benchmark HCD datasets. Source codes: https://github.com/IHCLab/HyperLUCID.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting，一个多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自治AI智能体并组织成分层嵌套结构，通过"应用即智能体"和"对话即集成"两个新范式解决传统企业应用集成（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在的高耦合、高运维成本和智能化不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业依赖多种异构业务系统和应用，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。传统集成方案（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升、智能化能力有限等固有缺陷，难以有效利用和编排已有企业应用。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架：(1) 将遗留应用提取为数字代理智能体，支持自然语言交互和自主操作；(2) 按层级监护拓扑组织智能体，而非扁平互联，以映射企业生态的组成复杂性；(3) 通过中央编排器协调多个智能体进行任务分解和动态调度；(4) 提供统一的对话式交互界面，实现跨应用查询和流程编排。

Result: 主要贡献包括提出"应用即智能体"（Application-as-Agent）集成范式和"对话即集成"（Conversation-as-Integration）交互理念，并探索了该方法在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用等场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting通过将企业应用智能体化并分层嵌套组织，为克服传统集成方案的局限性提供了新路径，有望在异构系统协调与大规模数据应用中展现良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [174] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"点火指数"(Ignition Index)，这是一个验证过的标量指标，用于操作化全局工作空间理论(GWT)在Transformer语言模型中的全有或全无点火预测。该指标通过对层内线性探针准确率拟合四参数Sigmoid函数并提取陡峭参数β-hat来量化模型信息处理从渐变到突变的转变程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论预测认知处理应表现出全有或全无的点火式转变，但此前缺乏能够定量验证这一预测的神经机制可解释性指标。现有研究未能将GWT的动态预测与语言模型的机制性解释有效桥接。

Method: 提出Ignition Index指标：对每层线性探针准确率依输入信号强度拟合四参数Sigmoid函数，提取陡峭度参数β-hat作为点火指数。在11个模型、5种架构家族上进行验证，使用打乱标签作为对照，并采用PELT算法检测训练过程中的相变。

Result: (1) 前馈Transformer比SSM聚合β-hat高出89%，Mamba呈现近线性剖面；(2) Huginn-3.5B在迭代轴上比深度轴点火高2.12倍；(3) Pythia-410M在训练第256步检测到相变(+67%)，先于归纳头形成；(4) 点火与模型规模及信号强度的假设未获确认。整体测量选择性达9.6倍。

Conclusion: Ignition Index首次提供了GWT动态预测与机制可解释性之间的验证性定量桥梁，具有9.6倍的测量选择性和此前缩放文献中未表征的架构级判别力，为理解不同架构中全局广播机制的差异提供了新工具。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [175] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation框架，通过对比弱模型在同一前缀上的成功与不成功局部补丁，构建纠正性教师分布并蒸馏到强模型中，以修复大语言模型在推理任务中的局部推理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务中经常失败，作者认为许多失败源于中间步骤的局部推理错误而非整体能力不足。这些错误通常可以通过插入弱模型的短补丁来修复，但直接微调这些补丁无法有效内化纠正效果，因为有用信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于它如何重塑模型的未来推理分布。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation训练框架：(1) 在同一前缀上为弱模型生成成功和不成功的局部补丁；(2) 对比这些补丁所引发的未来token预测，构建纠正性教师分布；(3) 将该信号蒸馏到强模型中。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation持续提升强模型性能，并优于直接模仿基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过对比式局部干预学习而非直接模仿修补文本，可以更有效地将弱到强的纠正信号内化到强模型中，从而修复推理过程中的局部错误。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [176] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI
