Abstract: Localising multiple sound sources in visual scenes remains a fundamental challenge in multimodal perception due to an inherent circular dependency: separating mixed audio requires knowing source locations, while identifying sound-producing regions requires separated audio signals. In this paper, we focus on the dual-source setting and discover a selective convergence in self-supervised audio-visual learning: when presented with multiple sound sources, contrastive models naturally converge to the most salient audio-visual correspondence rather than attempting to represent all sources equally. This emergent phenomenon, analogous to human selective auditory attention, enables us to break the above circular dependency through a progressive two-stage framework: first, leveraging selective convergence to identify dominant sources, and then exploiting these learned priors to uncover remaining sources. Our self-supervised approach achieves the best performance among self-supervised methods on dual-source benchmarks without requiring any manual annotations, and even surpasses some weakly-supervised approaches \red{on certain metrics. Furthermore, we identify a fundamental evaluation inconsistency in existing benchmarks: comparing continuous localisation heatmaps against bounding-box annotations creates systematic biases, particularly for non-axis-aligned objects where the bounding box includes substantial background regions. To address this, we introduce pixel-level segmentation masks to the existing benchmark, enabling spatially-aligned evaluation. Together, these results suggest that embracing rather than suppressing selectivity offers a scalable, annotation-free route to multi-source localisation.

</details>


### [277] [M$^3$Prune: Hierarchical Collaborative Pruning for Efficient Multi-Modal Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05967)
*Taolin Zhang,Weizi shao,Zijie Zhou,Chen Chen,Daiyang Yu,Tingyuan Hu,Chengyu Wang,Xiaofeng He*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出M3Prune框架，通过多模态多智能体分层通信图剪枝，在保持甚至提升多模态检索增强生成任务性能的同时，大幅降低token开销和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体的多模态检索增强生成（mRAG）系统虽然通过智能体间有效通信取得了优异性能，但存在巨大的token开销和计算成本，难以大规模部署，亟需一种既能保持性能又能提升通信效率的方法。

Method: M3Prune框架包含三个阶段：1）在文本和视觉模态内进行模态内图稀疏化，识别任务关键通信链路；2）构建模态间通信图，通过模态对齐分数稀疏化跨模态连接并鼓励一致的跨模态推理；3）逐步剪枝冗余边，获得高效的分层通信拓扑结构。

Result: 在通用领域和领域特定的mRAG基准上，M3Prune持续优于单智能体及强多智能体mRAG系统，同时显著提升了token效率。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态和模态内双重通信图剪枝，可以实现多智能体mRAG系统在性能与token效率之间的更优权衡，为多模态智能体系统的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-modal retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG), which augments multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) with external knowledge, have shown that collective intelligence from multiple agents can outperform a single model through effective communication. Despite their strong performance, existing multi-agent systems incur substantial token overhead and computational cost, posing challenges for large-scale deployment. To address these issues, we propose a Multi-Modal Multi-agent hierarchical communication graph PRUNING framework, termed M3Prune. M3Prune eliminates redundant communication edges both across and within modalities, improving the trade-off between task performance and token overhead. Specifically, M3Prune first performs intra-modal graph sparsification in the textual and visual modalities to identify task-critical communication links. It then constructs an inter-modal communication graph and sparsifies cross-modal connections while encouraging consistent cross-modal reasoning through a modality alignment score. Finally, it progressively prunes redundant edges to obtain an efficient hierarchical topology. Extensive experiments on both general-domain and domain-specific mRAG benchmarks show that M3Prune consistently outperforms single-agent and strong multi-agent mRAG systems while signifi- cantly improving token efficiency.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

### [278] [Velocity- and Regime-Aware Detection of Intraday Options Market Manipulation, with Explainable Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05373)
*Alex Chen,Maria Hybinette*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于市场状态速度（而非水平）的日内操纵检测方法，通过平滑状态速度（指数期权的Delta速度和股票价格速度）捕捉"泵-崩"动态特征。在印度BANKNIFTY指数期权测试集上，普通自编码器可恢复10/10的监管确认操纵日；在美国股票SEC v. Patel案例中，泵反转形状评分AUC达0.91（ARQQ）和0.81（ACY）。SHAP归因显示未确认告警与确认案件共享相同归因轮廓（余弦相似度0.99），精度上限一致，说明精度瓶颈源于执法标签不完整而非检测器失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日内市场操纵难以检测，因其足迹短暂、淹没在数百万条报价中，且与普通波动性统计上相似。现有检测器为达到高召回率不得不标记大量正常天数，导致精度崩溃，产生监管者无法行动的警报。作者旨在发现操纵的独特动态特征以改进检测。

Method: 构建分钟级检测流程，严格按时间划分训练/测试集，基于平滑状态速度（期权Delta速度与股票价格速度）进行检测；使用SHAP归因解释每个警报；通过隐马尔可夫模型推断市场机制进行条件检测（结果为负面）；采用"泵-反转"形状评分并将方法跨市场迁移。

Result: 在印度BANKNIFTY指数期权锁定测试集上，普通自编码器恢复10/10监管确认操纵日。HMM市场机制条件化检测为负面结果——虽然机制描述上有区分，但用其以召回换精度，封闭世界假设下精度仍约25%。在美国SEC v. Patel案例中，泵反转形状评分AUC为0.91（ARQQ）和0.81（ACY），ARQQ评分在投诉文档中的分钟窗口内达到峰值。SHAP归因显示未确认告警与确认日余弦相似度0.99。

Conclusion: 精度天花板与不完整的执法标签一致，而非检测器失效。跨市场和工具类型迁移的是动态特征本身——泵-崩/泵-反转模式的速度特征——而非具体幅度。该动态签名是日内操纵的普遍可迁移信号。

Abstract: Intraday market manipulation is hard to detect because its footprint is brief, buried in millions of quotes, and statistically similar to ordinary volatility. Detectors reach high recall only by flagging so many other days that measured precision collapses, producing alerts no regulator can act on. We show that this manipulation leaves a distinctive dynamic signature: a pump-and-crash pattern visible in the velocity of market state, rather than its level.
  We build a minute-level detection pipeline, strictly partitioned in time, based on smoothed state velocity: option-Delta velocity for index options and price velocity for equities. We explain every alert with SHAP attribution. We hold the test period strictly out-of-sample and fix all thresholds before evaluation. On the locked Indian BANKNIFTY index-options test, the plain autoencoder recovers 10 of 10 regulator-identified manipulation days.
  Conditioning detection on market regimes inferred by a hidden Markov model yields an instructive negative result. The regimes are descriptively distinct, but using them trades recall for precision. Under the closed-world assumption that unlabeled days are normal, precision remains near 25%.
  The same dynamic appears in thinly traded U.S. equities (SEC v. Patel). The shape of the signature survives the transfer; its velocity magnitude does not. A pump-reversal shape score ranks the complaint's alleged manipulation days with AUC 0.91 (ARQQ) and 0.81 (ACY). On the ARQQ worked example, the score peaks inside the complaint's documented minute window.
  Finally, exact SHAP attribution over every alert shows that unconfirmed alerts share the regulator-identified days' attribution profile (cosine similarity 0.99). The precision ceiling is consistent with incomplete enforcement labels rather than detector failure. What transfers across markets and instrument types is the dynamic signature itself.

</details>
